- **code-plan**: Converts code-audit findings into a dependency-ordered fix roadmap with phased deliverables. The battle-plan equivalent for code. Produces CQ-{phase}-{NNN} deliverables organized across four phases: Fix What's Broken → Close the Gaps → Structural Improvements → Harden & Prevent. Key difference from battle-plan: Phase 0 exit gate is OBJECTIVE — `pnpm typecheck` returns 0 errors, not "re-read and judge." Each deliverable routes to concrete verification commands.
